    16→func createSnowflakeResources(ctx *pulumi.Context) (*SnowflakeOutputs, error) {
    17→	// ===========================================
    18→	// Snowflake Database
    19→	// ===========================================
    20→	sfDatabase, err := snowflake.NewDatabase(ctx, "raw", &snowflake.DatabaseArgs{
    21→		Name: pulumi.String("RAW"),
    22→	})
    23→	if err != nil {
    24→		return nil, err
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	// ===========================================

    29→	// ===========================================
    30→	sfSchema, err := snowflake.NewSchema(ctx, "ingestion", &snowflake.SchemaArgs{
    31→		Database: sfDatabase.Name,
    32→		Name:     pulumi.String("INGESTION"),
    33→	})
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, err
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// ===========================================
    39→	// Snowflake Warehouse
    40→	// ===========================================
    41→	sfWarehouse, err := snowflake.NewWarehouse(ctx, "analytics", &snowflake.WarehouseArgs{
    42→		Name:          pulumi.String("ANALYTICS_WH"),
    43→		WarehouseSize: pulumi.String("XSMALL"),
    44→		AutoSuspend:   pulumi.Int(60),
    45→		AutoResume:    pulumi.String("true"),
    46→	})
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return nil, err
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	// ===========================================
    52→	// Snowflake Admin Database (for tools/management)
    53→	// ===========================================
    54→	adminDb, err := snowflake.NewDatabase(ctx, "admin", &snowflake.DatabaseArgs{
    55→		Name: pulumi.String("ADMIN"),
    56→	})
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, err
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// ===========================================
    62→	// Snowflake Git API Integration (Execute)
    63→	// ApiIntegration resource does not support git_https_api provider
    64→	// ===========================================
    65→	gitApiIntegration, err := snowflake.NewExecute(ctx, "gitApiIntegration", &snowflake.ExecuteArgs{
    66→		Execute: pulumi.String("CREATE OR REPLACE API INTEGRATION git_api_integration API_PROVIDER = git_https_api API_ALLOWED_PREFIXES = ('https://github.com/ta93abe') ENABLED = TRUE"),
    67→		Revert:  pulumi.String("DROP INTEGRATION IF EXISTS git_api_integration"),
    68→	})
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return nil, err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// ===========================================
    74→	// Snowflake Git Repository
    75→	// ===========================================
    76→	gitRepo, err := snowflake.NewGitRepository(ctx, "dataEngineering", &snowflake.GitRepositoryArgs{
    77→		Database:       adminDb.Name,
    78→		Schema:         pulumi.String("PUBLIC"),
    79→		Name:           pulumi.String("DATA_ENGINEERING"),
    80→		Origin:         pulumi.String("https://github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare.git"),
    81→		ApiIntegration: pulumi.String("GIT_API_INTEGRATION"),
    82→	}, pulumi.DependsOn([]pulumi.Resource{gitApiIntegration, adminDb}))
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return nil, err
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	return &SnowflakeOutputs{

    90→		WarehouseName:     sfWarehouse.Name,

    92→		GitRepositoryName: gitRepo.Name,

    94→}

> AGENT

現状を把握しました。整理すると:

**既存の Snowflake リソース** (`snowflake.go`):
- `RAW` データベース + `INGESTION` スキーマ
- `ANALYTICS_WH` ウェアハウス (XSMALL)
- `ADMIN` データベース + Git Repository

**dbt core の現状** (`profiles.yml`):
- 現在は **DuckDB** を使用中
- `staging` (view) + `marts` (table) のスキーマ構成

---

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
dbt では一般的に、RAW データが入るデータベースと dbt が書き込む先のデータベースを分離します。これにより「読み取り元」と「変換先」が明確になり、権限管理もシンプルになります。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## 設計提案

### 作成する Snowflake リソース

| リソース | 名前 | 用途 |
|---------|------|------|
| **Database** | `CORE` | dbt の変換結果を格納 |
| **Role** | `DBT_ROLE` | dbt サービスユーザー専用ロール |
| **Service User** | `DBT_SERVICE_USER` | GitHub Actions から WIF (OIDC) で認証 |
| **Warehouse** | `ANALYTICS_WH` | 既存を共用 |

### アーキテクチャ

```
GitHub Actions (OIDC Token)
     │
     ▼
DBT_SERVICE_USER (WIF auth)
     │
     ├─ DBT_ROLE が付与
     │    ├─ CORE データベース: ALL PRIVILEGES（staging/marts スキーマへ書き込み）
     │    ├─ RAW データベース: READ ONLY（ソースデータ読み取り）
     │    └─ ANALYTICS_WH: USAGE（クエリ実行）
     │
     └─ dbt run → CORE.STAGING / CORE.MARTS に書き込み
```

### アプローチ: WIF 設定方法

**推奨: Pulumi で Service User + Role + Grant を作成し、WIF 設定は `snowflake.NewExecute` で行う**

Pulumi の snowflake プロバイダーには `ServiceUser` や `WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` の直接サポートが限定的なため、ネイティブリソースと `Execute` (raw SQL) のハイブリッドで構成します。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Snowflake の `TYPE = SERVICE` ユーザーはパスワードレス前提。WIF や キーペアでのみ認証可能
- `WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` 認証は、ユーザーの `AUTHENTICATION_POLICY` ではなくユーザー作成時の `AUTHENTICATION_TYPE` で設定
- Pulumi snowflake プロバイダー v2 は ServiceUser リソースがあるが、WIF 関連パラメータのサポートはまだ限定的な場合がある
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

### 権限設計の詳細

```sql
-- DBT_ROLE の権限
GRANT USAGE ON WAREHOUSE ANALYTICS_WH TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE CORE TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;
GRANT USAGE ON DATABASE RAW TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;
GRANT USAGE ON ALL SCHEMAS IN DATABASE RAW TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN DATABASE RAW TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;
GRANT SELECT ON FUTURE TABLES IN DATABASE RAW TO ROLE DBT_ROLE;

-- Service User
CREATE USER DBT_SERVICE_USER
  TYPE = SERVICE
  DEFAULT_ROLE = DBT_ROLE
  DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE = ANALYTICS_WH;
GRANT ROLE DBT_ROLE TO USER DBT_SERVICE_USER;
```

### 4. ServiceUser WIF / Workload Identity Federation Support -- YES

The `DefaultWorkloadIdentity` field on `ServiceUserArgs` fully supports WIF. Here are the **exact struct definitions** from the source:

**Important caveat from the source code comment (line 917 in serviceUser.go):**
> This field can be only used when `USER_ENABLE_DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` option is specified in provider block in the `experimentalFeaturesEnabled` field. If this feature is not enabled, attempting to set this field will result in an error.

```go
type ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityArgs struct {
    Aws   ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityAwsPtrInput   `pulumi:"aws"`
    Azure ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityAzurePtrInput `pulumi:"azure"`
    Gcp   ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityGcpPtrInput   `pulumi:"gcp"`
    Oidc  ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityOidcPtrInput  `pulumi:"oidc"`
}
```

#### AWS

```go
type ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityAwsArgs struct {
    // The ARN of the AWS IAM role to use for workload identity federation.
    Arn pulumi.StringInput `pulumi:"arn"`
}
```

#### Azure

```go
type ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityAzureArgs struct {
    // The Azure issuer URL.
    Issuer  pulumi.StringInput `pulumi:"issuer"`
    // The Azure subject identifier.
    Subject pulumi.StringInput `pulumi:"subject"`
}
```

#### GCP

```go
type ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityGcpArgs struct {
    // The GCP service account subject identifier.
    Subject pulumi.StringInput `pulumi:"subject"`
}
```

#### OIDC (Generic)

```go
type ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityOidcArgs struct {
    // The OIDC issuer URL.
    Issuer            pulumi.StringInput      `pulumi:"issuer"`
    // List of allowed OIDC audiences.
    OidcAudienceLists pulumi.StringArrayInput  `pulumi:"oidcAudienceLists"`
    // The OIDC subject identifier.
    Subject           pulumi.StringInput      `pulumi:"subject"`
}
```

#### Example Usage (OIDC WIF for GitHub Actions)

```go
svcUser, err := snowflake.NewServiceUser(ctx, "github-actions-user", &snowflake.ServiceUserArgs{
    Name:        pulumi.String("GITHUB_ACTIONS_SVC"),
    LoginName:   pulumi.String("GITHUB_ACTIONS_SVC"),
    DefaultRole: pulumi.String("GITHUB_ACTIONS_ROLE"),
    DefaultWorkloadIdentity: &snowflake.ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityArgs{
        Oidc: &snowflake.ServiceUserDefaultWorkloadIdentityOidcArgs{
            Issuer:            pulumi.String("https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com"),
            Subject:           pulumi.String("repo:org/repo:ref:refs/heads/main"),
            OidcAudienceLists: pulumi.StringArray{pulumi.String("snowflakecomputing.com")},
        },
    },
})
```

**Provider config requirement** -- you must enable the experimental feature:

```go
provider, err := snowflake.NewProvider(ctx, "snowflake", &snowflake.ProviderArgs{
    // ... other config ...
    ExperimentalFeaturesEnabled: pulumi.StringArray{
        pulumi.String("USER_ENABLE_DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY"),
    },
})
```

- GitHub Actions の OIDC issuer は `https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com`、audience は `snowflakecomputing.com`

     1→name: Pulumi Deploy
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  push:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    paths:
     7→      - "infrastructure/pulumi/**"
     8→      - ".github/workflows/pulumi-deploy.yml"
     9→
    10→env:
    11→  CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
    12→  CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
    13→  AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID }}
    14→  AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: ${{ secrets.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY }}
    15→  PULUMI_CONFIG_PASSPHRASE: ${{ secrets.PULUMI_CONFIG_PASSPHRASE }}
    16→  SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME }}
    17→  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME }}
    18→  SNOWFLAKE_USER: PULUMI_SVC
    19→  SNOWFLAKE_AUTHENTICATOR: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY
    20→  SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER: OIDC
    21→  SNOWFLAKE_ROLE: PULUMI_ROLE
    22→
    23→jobs:
    24→  deploy:
    25→    name: Deploy
    26→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    27→    environment: pulumi
    28→    permissions:
    29→      id-token: write
    30→      contents: read
    31→    defaults:
    32→      run:
    33→        working-directory: infrastructure/pulumi
    34→
    35→    steps:
    36→      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
    37→
    38→      - uses: actions/setup-go@v5
    39→        with:
    40→          go-version: "1.24"
    41→          cache-dependency-path: infrastructure/pulumi/go.sum
    42→
    43→      - name: Install dependencies
    44→        run: go mod tidy
    45→
    46→      - name: Get Snowflake OIDC token
    47→        run: |
    48→          OIDC_TOKEN=$(curl -sS -H "Authorization: bearer $ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN" \
    49→            "${ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_URL}&audience=snowflakecomputing.com" | jq -r '.value')
    50→          if [ -z "$OIDC_TOKEN" ] || [ "$OIDC_TOKEN" = "null" ]; then
    51→            echo "::error::Failed to retrieve Snowflake OIDC token"
    52→            exit 1
    53→          fi
    54→          echo "::add-mask::$OIDC_TOKEN"
    55→          echo "SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN=$OIDC_TOKEN" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
    56→
    57→      - name: Login to R2 backend
    58→        run: pulumi login 's3://pulumi?endpoint=https://${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com&region=auto&s3ForcePathStyle=true'
    59→
    60→      - name: Select stack
    61→        run: pulumi stack select dev
    62→
    63→      - name: Pulumi Up
    64→        run: pulumi up --yes --refresh
    65→
```

- 既存の `PULUMI_SVC` は WIF (OIDC) で GitHub Actions から認証済み
- Pulumi snowflake v2 は `NewServiceUser` で `DefaultWorkloadIdentity.Oidc` をサポート（ただし experimental feature flag が必要）
- 既存コードは `NewExecute` で raw SQL も使用するパターンあり（git API integration）

```
     1→# Snowflake dbt Resources Design
     2→
     3→## Overview
     4→
     5→dbt core プロジェクト (`transform/core/`) を Snowflake で実行するために必要なリソースを Pulumi で管理する。
     6→
     7→## Decisions
     8→
     9→| Decision | Choice | Rationale |
    10→|----------|--------|-----------|
    11→| Database name | `CORE` | dbt project name と一致 |
    12→| Warehouse | `ANALYTICS_WH` (既存共用) | 個人プロジェクトでリソース節約 |
    13→| Auth method | WIF (OIDC) | GitHub Actions のみで実行。パスワードレスでセキュア |
    14→| Role | `DBT_ROLE` | 最小権限の原則に従った専用ロール |
    15→
    16→## Architecture
    17→
    18→```
    19→GitHub Actions (OIDC Token)
    20→     |
    21→     v
    22→DBT_SERVICE_USER (WIF auth, TYPE=SERVICE)
    23→     |
    24→     +-- DBT_ROLE
    25→     |    +-- CORE database: ALL PRIVILEGES (staging/marts schema)
    26→     |    +-- RAW database: READ ONLY (source data)
    27→     |    +-- ANALYTICS_WH: USAGE
    28→     |
    29→     +-- dbt run -> CORE.STAGING / CORE.MARTS
    30→```
    31→
    32→## Resources to Create
    33→
    34→### 1. Database: CORE
    35→
    36→dbt の変換結果を格納するデータベース。staging/marts スキーマは dbt が自動作成する。
    37→
    38→### 2. Role: DBT_ROLE
    39→
    40→Grants:
    41→- `USAGE ON WAREHOUSE ANALYTICS_WH`
    42→- `ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE CORE`
    43→- `USAGE ON DATABASE RAW`
    44→- `USAGE ON ALL SCHEMAS IN DATABASE RAW`
    45→- `SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN DATABASE RAW`
    46→- `SELECT ON FUTURE TABLES IN DATABASE RAW`
    47→
    48→### 3. Service User: DBT_SERVICE_USER
    49→
    50→- `TYPE = SERVICE` (passwordless)
    51→- `DEFAULT_ROLE = DBT_ROLE`
    52→- `DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE = ANALYTICS_WH`
    53→- WIF (WORKLOAD_IDENTITY) authentication via GitHub Actions OIDC
    54→
    55→### 4. Warehouse: ANALYTICS_WH (existing, shared)
    56→
    57→No changes needed. Already XSMALL with auto-suspend=60s.
    58→
    59→## Implementation Notes
    60→
    61→- Pulumi snowflake provider v2 の `ServiceUser` リソースと `AccountRole` / Grant リソースを使用
    62→- WIF 設定は `snowflake.NewExecute` で raw SQL を使用する可能性あり（プロバイダーのサポート状況次第）
    63→- dbt profiles.yml に Snowflake target を追加する必要がある（別タスク）
    64→
```

計画が完成し、`docs/plans/2026-02-17-snowflake-dbt-resources-design.md` に保存しました。

---

**計画のサマリー:**

| Task | 内容 | リソース数 |
|------|------|-----------|
| Task 1 | CORE DB + DBT_ROLE 作成 | 2 |
| Task 2 | DBT_ROLE への権限付与 | 7 |
| Task 3 | DBT_SERVICE_USER + WIF + ロール付与 | 3 |
| Task 4 | 全体検証 | - |

合計 **12 リソース** を Pulumi で作成します。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- WIF の `NewExecute` アプローチは、experimental feature flag を避けつつ既存コード（git API integration）と同じパターンを踏襲
- `DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` の subject に `environment:dbt` を使用するため、GitHub に `dbt` environment を作成する必要がある（デプロイ時）
- `ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE` だけでは dbt がスキーマを作れない場合があるため、`CREATE SCHEMA` 権限も明示的に追加
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

---

**実行方法は2つあります:**

1. **Subagent-Driven (このセッション)** - タスクごとにサブエージェントを起動し、レビューしながら進行
2. **Parallel Session (別セッション)** - 新しいセッションで executing-plans を使って一括実行

どちらがよいですか？

     3→Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
     4→

     6→Task tool (general-purpose):
     7→  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
     8→  prompt: |
     9→    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
    10→
    11→    ## Task Description
    12→
    13→    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]

    15→    ## Context
    16→
    17→    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
    18→

    20→
    21→    If you have questions about:
    22→    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
    23→    - The approach or implementation strategy
    24→    - Dependencies or assumptions
    25→    - Anything unclear in the task description
    26→
    27→    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
    28→

Perfect! Now I have all the context I need. Let me prepare a comprehensive code review.

---

## Code Review: Add CORE Database and DBT_ROLE for dbt

### Summary

This commit adds Snowflake resources required for dbt transformations: a CORE database and a DBT_ROLE for service user authentication. The changes follow the established patterns in the codebase and maintain good code quality.

### Strengths

1. **Consistent Pattern Adherence**: The implementation follows the exact same pattern as existing Snowflake resources (RAW database, ADMIN database):
   - Proper section headers with visual separation (`// ===...===`)
   - Consistent error handling with immediate return
   - Resources exported through a struct

2. **Complete Struct Updates**: Both the `SnowflakeOutputs` struct and return statement were properly updated:
   - New fields added in logical order after AdminDatabaseName
   - Corresponding outputs added to the struct initialization
   - Exports properly placed in main.go

3. **Proper Resource Comments**: Each resource has clear documentation:
   - Database comment: "Snowflake Database for dbt"
   - Role comment: "Role for dbt service user to run transformations"

4. **Error Handling**: Standard Go error handling pattern applied consistently with immediate returns

5. **Naming Conventions**: Follows Go conventions and codebase standards:
   - Snake_case for Snowflake resource names (CORE, DBT_ROLE)
   - camelCase for Go variables (coreDb, dbtRole)
   - Descriptive resource logical IDs ("core", "dbtRole")

### Issues

#### Important: Missing Role-to-Database Grants

**Severity**: Important (should fix)

**Issue**: The DBT_ROLE is created but has no explicit grants assigned to access the CORE database. For dbt to function correctly, the role needs specific privileges.

**Context**: This is a common issue in Snowflake IaC implementations. Unlike some cloud databases, Snowflake requires explicit grant statements.

**Location**: `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go`, lines 76-82

**Impact**: The role cannot perform dbt operations without grants, making the deployment incomplete. Typical required grants include:
- USAGE on CORE database
- USAGE on schemas within CORE
- CREATE/SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE on tables

**Recommendation**: Add grant resources using `snowflake.GrantDatabaseRole` or `snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToDatabaseRole`. Example pattern:

```go
// Grant privileges to DBT_ROLE on CORE database
dbtDbGrant, err := snowflake.NewGrantPrivilegesToDatabaseRole(ctx, "dbtCoreGrant", &snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToDatabaseRoleArgs{
	DatabaseRoleName: dbtRole.Name,
	Privileges:       pulumi.StringArray{pulumi.String("USAGE")},
	Database:         coreDb.Name,
})
if err != nil {
	return nil, err
}
```

#### Suggestion: Missing Service User Creation

**Severity**: Suggestion (nice to have, but related)

**Issue**: While DBT_ROLE is created, there's no corresponding DBT_SERVICE_USER referenced in the project memory. The role needs to be assigned to a user for authentication to work.

**Note**: This may be intentionally deferred to a subsequent commit (Task 3 in the visible task list references "Add DBT_SERVICE_USER with WIF"), so this is more of a documentation note.

**Recommendation**: Consider documenting in a comment that the user assignment is handled separately, or verify this is expected.

#### Minor: Database Creation Without Additional Config

**Severity**: Suggestion (minor)

**Issue**: The CORE database is created with minimal configuration (only Name parameter). While this is valid, it may benefit from future configurations like:
- Comment for documentation
- Data retention days settings
- Tag assignments for cost tracking

**Current State** (lines 66-71):
```go
coreDb, err := snowflake.NewDatabase(ctx, "core", &snowflake.DatabaseArgs{
	Name: pulumi.String("CORE"),
})
```

**Recommendation**: Consider adding documentation comment in the Args:

```go
coreDb, err := snowflake.NewDatabase(ctx, "core", &snowflake.DatabaseArgs{
	Name:    pulumi.String("CORE"),
	Comment: pulumi.String("Database for dbt transformation models"),
})
```

This provides better infrastructure documentation.

### Architecture & Design Assessment

**Pattern Consistency**: Excellent. The code fits seamlessly into the existing Pulumi Go module structure.

**Resource Ordering**: Logical placement after ADMIN database and before Git integration, which follows the resource dependency flow.

**Export Strategy**: Properly exports outputs through the main function, enabling downstream consumption in scripts or other infrastructure components.

### Code Quality Checklist

- [x] Error handling present and consistent
- [x] Naming conventions followed
- [x] Comments and documentation present
- [x] Pattern consistency with existing code
- [x] Type safety maintained
- [x] No security vulnerabilities evident
- [x] Struct definitions updated appropriately
- [ ] Dependent resources (grants) missing

---

## Assessment

**Status**: **Changes Needed**

The implementation is solid and follows all established patterns, but the **missing role grants are critical for functionality**. The DBT_ROLE cannot perform its intended operations without explicit privileges on the CORE database.

**Action Required**:
1. Add grant statements to assign USAGE and transformation privileges to DBT_ROLE on CORE database
2. Verify grant configuration matches dbt service account requirements
3. Optional: Add database comment for better documentation

Once grants are added, the implementation will be complete and production-ready.

## Report

### What I implemented

Added 7 `GrantPrivilegesToAccountRole` resources for `DBT_ROLE` in the Snowflake Pulumi infrastructure. These grants provide the minimum necessary permissions for a dbt service user to read from the RAW database and write transformations to the CORE database.

The 7 grants, in order:

1. **`dbtGrantWarehouse`** -- USAGE on ANALYTICS_WH warehouse (allows dbt to run queries)
2. **`dbtGrantCoreDb`** -- ALL PRIVILEGES on CORE database (full control for dbt's output database)
3. **`dbtGrantRawDbUsage`** -- USAGE on RAW database (allows dbt to connect to the source database)
4. **`dbtGrantRawSchemasUsage`** -- USAGE on all schemas in RAW (allows dbt to access existing schemas)
5. **`dbtGrantRawTablesSelect`** -- SELECT on all existing tables in RAW (allows dbt to read source data)
6. **`dbtGrantRawFutureTablesSelect`** -- SELECT on future tables in RAW (ensures new tables are automatically readable)
7. **`dbtGrantCoreCreateSchema`** -- CREATE SCHEMA on CORE (allows dbt to create staging/marts schemas)

### Files changed

- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go` -- 98 lines added (lines 84-180)

### Build verification

`go build ./...` completed successfully with no errors.

### Self-review findings

- All 7 grants are placed correctly between the `dbtRole` creation (line 82) and the Git API Integration section (line 182).
- Each grant uses `.FullyQualifiedName` for referencing resources (not `.Name`), which is the correct approach for grant arguments in the Pulumi Snowflake provider.
- Each grant has proper error handling (`if err != nil { return nil, err }`).
- The section header comment (`// Grants for DBT_ROLE`) clearly delineates the new block.
- Resource names are descriptive and follow a consistent `dbtGrant*` naming pattern.

### Issues or concerns

None. The implementation matches the specification exactly and the build compiles cleanly.

Here is the report:

## What I Implemented

Added 3 Pulumi resources for the `DBT_SERVICE_USER` with Workload Identity Federation (OIDC) authentication:

1. **`snowflake.NewServiceUser`** (`dbtServiceUser`) -- Creates the `DBT_SERVICE_USER` service account with default role `DBT_ROLE`, default warehouse `ANALYTICS_WH`, and default namespace `CORE`. Depends on `dbtRole`, `sfWarehouse`, and `coreDb`.

2. **`snowflake.NewExecute`** (`dbtUserWif`) -- Configures WIF/OIDC on the service user via `ALTER USER ... SET DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY`, scoped to the GitHub Actions OIDC issuer with subject `repo:ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare:environment:dbt` and audience `snowflakecomputing.com`. The revert command unsets the identity. Depends on `dbtUser`.

3. **`snowflake.NewGrantAccountRole`** (`dbtGrantRoleToUser`) -- Grants the `DBT_ROLE` to `DBT_SERVICE_USER` so the user can assume the role at login.

Additionally:
- Added `DbtServiceUserName pulumi.StringOutput` to the `SnowflakeOutputs` struct
- Added `DbtServiceUserName: dbtUser.Name` to the return statement
- Added `ctx.Export("snowflakeDbtServiceUserName", sf.DbtServiceUserName)` to `main.go`
- Fixed struct field alignment for consistency across all fields

## Files Changed

- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go` -- Added 3 resources, 1 struct field, 1 return field (lines 183-222 for resources, line 15 for struct, line 257 for return)
- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/main.go` -- Added 1 export line (line 36)

## Build Verification

`go build ./...` passed with zero errors both before and after the alignment cleanup.

## Self-Review Findings

- All 3 resources are placed correctly: after the grants section (line 181) and before the Git API Integration section (line 224)
- Dependency chain is correct: `dbtUser` depends on `dbtRole`, `sfWarehouse`, `coreDb`; WIF execute depends on `dbtUser`
- The `NewGrantAccountRole` correctly uses `.Name` (not `.FullyQualifiedName`) for `RoleName` and `UserName`, consistent with how it differs from `GrantPrivilegesToAccountRole`
- The WIF OIDC syntax matches the Snowflake-specific `DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` format with proper escaping
- The `pulumi.Sprintf` correctly interpolates the user name into the SQL statement
- The export name `snowflakeDbtServiceUserName` follows the existing naming convention

## Issues or Concerns

None. The implementation matches the task specification exactly. The commit is `898546a`.

> AGENT

全タスク完了です。実装の全体像をまとめます。

---

## 完了サマリー

3つのコミットで **12 の Snowflake リソース**を Pulumi で追加しました:

| Commit | 内容 | リソース |
|--------|------|---------|
| `9b5bf62` | CORE DB + DBT_ROLE | Database, AccountRole |
| `7f07e7d` | DBT_ROLE への権限付与 | 7 Grants (WH USAGE, CORE ALL, RAW READ, CREATE SCHEMA) |
| `898546a` | サービスユーザー + WIF | ServiceUser, Execute(WIF), GrantAccountRole |

### 作成されるリソース構成

```
CORE (Database)         ← dbt の書き込み先 (staging/marts)
DBT_ROLE (Role)         ← 最小権限の専用ロール
  ├─ USAGE on ANALYTICS_WH
  ├─ ALL PRIVILEGES on CORE
  ├─ CREATE SCHEMA on CORE
  ├─ USAGE on RAW + schemas
  └─ SELECT on RAW tables (現在 + future)
DBT_SERVICE_USER        ← TYPE=SERVICE, WIF (OIDC) 認証
  ├─ DEFAULT_ROLE = DBT_ROLE
  ├─ DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE = ANALYTICS_WH
  └─ OIDC: GitHub Actions environment:dbt
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `pulumi up` でデプロイする前に、GitHub に `dbt` environment を作成する必要があります（WIF の subject に `environment:dbt` を指定しているため）
- dbt profiles.yml への Snowflake target 追加は次のステップとして別途対応が必要です
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

dbt environment に必要なのは同じ Snowflake 接続情報です:

| Secret | 用途 |
|--------|------|
| `SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME` | Snowflake org 名 |
| `SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME` | Snowflake account 名 |

ユーザー名 (`DBT_SERVICE_USER`) やロール (`DBT_ROLE`) は workflow ファイルにハードコードする想定なので secrets 不要です。

> AGENT

完了です。追加した内容:

**profiles.yml** - `snowflake` target:
```yaml
snowflake:
  type: snowflake
  account: "{SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME}-{SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME}"
  user: DBT_SERVICE_USER
  role: DBT_ROLE
  authenticator: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY
  token: $SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN
  warehouse: ANALYTICS_WH
  database: CORE
  schema: public
```

**pyproject.toml** - `dbt-snowflake>=1.9.0` を追加

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- GitHub Actions では `--target snowflake` で Snowflake target を指定して実行する形
- ローカル開発は引き続き `dev` (DuckDB) が default target のまま
- `SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER=OIDC` も workflow の env に設定が必要
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

id: toolu_01AJAJUuCDBqXUfQQcvb7NhH
```
name: "dbt Jobs"
description: "Run dbt CI / merge / deploy jobs as a composite action"

branding:
  icon: "play-circle"
  color: "purple"

inputs:
  type:
    description: "Job type: ci, merge, or deploy"
    required: true
  adapter:
    description: "dbt adapter short name (currently: snowflake)"
    required: true
  command:
    description: "dbt commands to run (multi-line supported)"
    required: true
  project-dir:
    description: "Path to the dbt project root"
    required: false
    default: "."
  profile-dir:
    description: "Directory containing profiles.yml"
    required: false
    default: "."
  deferral:
    description: "Branch to fetch production manifest from"
    required: false
    default: ""
  target:
    description: "dbt target to use"
    required: false
    default: ""
  run-timeout:
    description: "Timeout in seconds per command (0 = unlimited)"
    required: false
    default: "0"
  dbt-version:
    description: "dbt-core version (empty or 'latest' = auto-resolve)"
    required: false
    default: ""
  threads:
    description: "Number of dbt threads"
    required: false
    default: ""
  source-freshness:
    description: "Run 'dbt source freshness'"
    required: false
    default: "false"
  post-pr-comment:
    description: "Post build results as PR comment (ci only)"
    required: false
    default: "false"
  ci-schema-prefix:
    description: "Schema prefix for CI. Sets SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA to <prefix>_<PR number>."
    required: false
    default: "dbt_pr_job"

runs:
  using: "composite"
  steps:
    # ---- Step 1: Validate type ----
    - name: Validate type
      shell: bash
      env:
        INPUT_TYPE: ${{ inputs.type }}
      run: |
        if [[ "$INPUT_TYPE" != "ci" && "$INPUT_TYPE" != "merge" && "$INPUT_TYPE" != "deploy" ]]; then
          echo "::error::Invalid type '${INPUT_TYPE}'. Must be one of: ci, merge, deploy"
          exit 1
        fi

    # ---- Step 1.5: Override schema for CI ----
    - name: Override schema for CI
      if: inputs.type == 'ci' && inputs.ci-schema-prefix != ''
      shell: bash
      env:
        CI_SCHEMA_PREFIX: ${{ inputs.ci-schema-prefix }}
        PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
      run: |
        if [[ -z "$PR_NUMBER" ]]; then
          echo "::error::ci-schema-prefix requires a pull_request event"
          exit 1
        fi
        schema="${CI_SCHEMA_PREFIX}_${PR_NUMBER}"
        if [[ "$schema" == *$'\n'* ]]; then
          echo "::error::Schema name must not contain newlines"
          exit 1
        fi
        echo "SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA=${schema}" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
        echo "CI schema: ${schema}"

    # ---- Step 2: Setup dbt ----
    - name: Setup dbt
      uses: ./actions/setup
      with:
        adapter: ${{ inputs.adapter }}
        dbt-version: ${{ inputs.dbt-version == 'latest' && '' || inputs.dbt-version }}
        project-dir: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}

    # ---- Step 3: dbt deps ----
    - name: dbt deps
      shell: bash
      env:
        DBT_PROJECT_DIR: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}
        DBT_PROFILE_DIR: ${{ inputs.profile-dir }}
      run: |
        dbt deps --project-dir "$DBT_PROJECT_DIR" --profiles-dir "$DBT_PROFILE_DIR"

    # ---- Step 4: Download manifest ----
    - name: Download production manifest
      if: inputs.deferral != ''
      id: download-manifest
      uses: actions/github-script@v8
      env:
        DEFERRAL_BRANCH: ${{ inputs.deferral }}
      with:
        script: |
          const fs = require('fs');
          const branch = process.env.DEFERRAL_BRANCH;

          const runs = await github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRunsForRepo({
            owner: context.repo.owner,
            repo: context.repo.repo,
            branch,
            status: 'success',
            per_page: 5,
          });

          let artifact = null;
          for (const run of runs.data.workflow_runs) {
            const arts = await github.rest.actions.listWorkflowRunArtifacts({
              owner: context.repo.owner,
              repo: context.repo.repo,
              run_id: run.id,
            });
            artifact = arts.data.artifacts.find(a => a.name === 'dbt-artifacts' && !a.expired);
            if (artifact) break;
          }

          if (!artifact) {
            core.warning(`Artifact "dbt-artifacts" not found on branch "${branch}"`);
            core.setOutput('found', 'false');
            return;
          }

          const download = await github.rest.actions.downloadArtifact({
            owner: context.repo.owner,
            repo: context.repo.repo,
            artifact_id: artifact.id,
            archive_format: 'zip',
          });

          fs.writeFileSync('prod-manifest.zip', Buffer.from(download.data));
          core.setOutput('found', 'true');
      continue-on-error: true

    # ---- Step 5: Extract manifest ----
    - name: Extract production manifest
      if: steps.download-manifest.outputs.found == 'true'
      shell: bash
      run: |
        mkdir -p prod-manifest
        unzip -o prod-manifest.zip -d prod-manifest
        rm prod-manifest.zip

    # ---- Step 6: Execute commands ----
    - name: Execute dbt commands
      id: dbt-run
      shell: bash
      env:
        DBT_COMMAND: ${{ inputs.command }}
        DBT_PROFILES_DIR: ${{ inputs.profile-dir }}
        DBT_PROJECT_DIR: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}
        DBT_TARGET: ${{ inputs.target }}
        DBT_THREADS: ${{ inputs.threads }}
        DBT_TIMEOUT: ${{ inputs.run-timeout }}
        DBT_TYPE: ${{ inputs.type }}
        DBT_DEFERRAL: ${{ inputs.deferral }}
        DBT_MANIFEST_FOUND: ${{ steps.download-manifest.outputs.found }}
      run: |
        build_failed=0

        run_dbt_command() {
          local cmd="$1"
          CMD="$cmd" python3 - <<'PY'
        import os
        import shlex
        import subprocess
        import sys

        ALLOWED = frozenset({
            "build", "run", "test", "seed", "snapshot",
            "compile", "parse", "deps", "ls", "list",
            "source", "freshness", "show", "retry", "clone",
        })

        cmd = os.environ.get("CMD", "")
        timeout_text = os.environ.get("DBT_TIMEOUT", "0")
        profiles_dir = os.environ.get("DBT_PROFILES_DIR", "")
        project_dir = os.environ.get("DBT_PROJECT_DIR", "")
        target = os.environ.get("DBT_TARGET", "")
        threads = os.environ.get("DBT_THREADS", "")
        deferral = os.environ.get("DBT_DEFERRAL", "")
        manifest_found = os.environ.get("DBT_MANIFEST_FOUND", "")

        try:
            timeout = int(timeout_text)
        except ValueError:
            print(f"::error::run-timeout must be an integer, got: {timeout_text}")
            sys.exit(2)

        if timeout < 0:
            print(f"::error::run-timeout must be >= 0, got: {timeout}")
            sys.exit(2)

        try:
            args = shlex.split(cmd, posix=True)
        except ValueError as exc:
            print(f"::error::Invalid command syntax: {exc}")
            sys.exit(2)

        if not args or args[0] != "dbt":
            print("::error::Only dbt commands are allowed.")
            sys.exit(2)

        subcmd = next((a for a in args[1:] if not a.startswith("-")), None)
        if subcmd is None or subcmd not in ALLOWED:
            print(f"::error::Subcommand not allowed: {subcmd or '(none)'}")
            sys.exit(2)

        if profiles_dir and "--profiles-dir" not in args:
            args += ["--profiles-dir", profiles_dir]
        if project_dir and "--project-dir" not in args:
            args += ["--project-dir", project_dir]
        if target and "--target" not in args:
            args += ["--target", target]
        if threads and "--threads" not in args:
            args += ["--threads", threads]
        if deferral and manifest_found == "true" and "state:" in cmd and "--defer" not in args:
            args += ["--defer", "--state", "./prod-manifest"]

        print(f"Running: {' '.join(shlex.quote(a) for a in args)}")

        try:
            completed = subprocess.run(args, check=False, timeout=(timeout or None))
            sys.exit(completed.returncode)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            print(f"::error::Command timed out after {timeout} seconds")
            sys.exit(124)
        PY
        }

        while IFS= read -r line; do
          line="$(echo "$line" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')"
          [[ -z "$line" || "$line" == \#* ]] && continue

          echo "::group::$line"
          run_dbt_command "$line" && rc=0 || rc=$?
          echo "::endgroup::"

          if [[ $rc -ne 0 ]]; then
            if [[ "$DBT_TYPE" == "ci" ]]; then
              build_failed=1
              echo "::warning::Command failed (exit $rc): $line"
            else
              exit $rc
            fi
          fi
        done <<< "$DBT_COMMAND"

        echo "build-failed=$build_failed" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

    # ---- Step 7: Source freshness ----
    - name: dbt source freshness
      if: inputs.source-freshness == 'true'
      shell: bash
      env:
        DBT_PROFILE_DIR: ${{ inputs.profile-dir }}
        DBT_PROJECT_DIR: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}
        DBT_TARGET: ${{ inputs.target }}
        DBT_THREADS: ${{ inputs.threads }}
      run: |
        cmd=(dbt source freshness)
        cmd+=(--profiles-dir "$DBT_PROFILE_DIR")
        cmd+=(--project-dir "$DBT_PROJECT_DIR")
        if [[ -n "$DBT_TARGET" ]]; then
          cmd+=(--target "$DBT_TARGET")
        fi
        if [[ -n "$DBT_THREADS" ]]; then
          cmd+=(--threads "$DBT_THREADS")
        fi
        "${cmd[@]}"

    # ---- Step 8: Upload artifacts ----
    - name: Upload artifacts
      if: inputs.type == 'merge' || inputs.type == 'deploy'
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v6
      with:
        name: dbt-artifacts
        path: |
          ${{ inputs.project-dir }}/target/manifest.json
          ${{ inputs.project-dir }}/target/run_results.json
        retention-days: 90

    # ---- Step 10: PR comment ----
    - name: Post PR comment
      if: >-
        always()
        && inputs.type == 'ci'
        && inputs.post-pr-comment == 'true'
        && github.event_name == 'pull_request'
        && steps.dbt-run.outcome == 'success'
      uses: actions/github-script@v8
      env:
        DBT_COMMAND: ${{ inputs.command }}
        DBT_PROJECT_DIR: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}
        BUILD_FAILED: ${{ steps.dbt-run.outputs.build-failed }}
      with:
        script: |
          const fs = require('fs');
          const path = require('path');

          const projectDir = process.env.DBT_PROJECT_DIR;
          const runResultsPath = path.join(projectDir, 'target', 'run_results.json');
          const buildFailed = process.env.BUILD_FAILED === '1';
          const command = process.env.DBT_COMMAND;

          let body = '';

          if (fs.existsSync(runResultsPath)) {
            const results = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(runResultsPath, 'utf8'));
            const stats = { pass: 0, warn: 0, error: 0, skip: 0 };
            for (const r of results.results) {
              const status = r.status.toLowerCase();
              if (status === 'pass' || status === 'success') stats.pass++;
              else if (status === 'warn') stats.warn++;
              else if (status === 'error' || status === 'fail') stats.error++;
              else stats.skip++;
            }

            const icon = stats.error > 0 ? ':x:' : ':white_check_mark:';
            body = [
              `## ${icon} dbt Build Results`,
              '',
              '**Command:**',
              '```',
              command.trim(),
              '```',
              '',
              '| Status | Count |',
              '|--------|-------|',
              `| :white_check_mark: Pass | ${stats.pass} |`,
              `| :warning: Warn | ${stats.warn} |`,
              `| :x: Error | ${stats.error} |`,
              `| :fast_forward: Skip | ${stats.skip} |`,
              '',
              '<details><summary>Details</summary>',
              '',
              '| Node | Status | Time |',
              '|------|--------|------|',
              ...results.results.map(r => {
                const statusIcon = (r.status === 'pass' || r.status === 'success') ? ':white_check_mark:' : r.status === 'error' || r.status === 'fail' ? ':x:' : ':warning:';
                return `| \`${r.unique_id}\` | ${statusIcon} ${r.status} | ${r.execution_time.toFixed(2)}s |`;
              }),
              '',
              '</details>',
            ].join('\n');
          } else {
            const status = buildFailed ? 'failure' : 'success';
            body = `## :warning: dbt Build\n\nNo run results found. Build status: **${status}**`;
          }

          const marker = '<!-- dbt-jobs -->';
          body = `${marker}\n${body}`;

          const { data: comments } = await github.rest.issues.listComments({
            owner: context.repo.owner,
            repo: context.repo.repo,
            issue_number: context.issue.number,
          });

          const existing = comments.find(c => c.body.startsWith(marker));
          if (existing) {
            await github.rest.issues.updateComment({
              owner: context.repo.owner,
              repo: context.repo.repo,
              comment_id: existing.id,
              body,
            });
          } else {
            await github.rest.issues.createComment({
              owner: context.repo.owner,
              repo: context.repo.repo,
              issue_number: context.issue.number,
              body,
            });
          }

    # ---- Step 11: Check build result ----
    - name: Check build result
      if: >-
        always()
        && inputs.type == 'ci'
        && steps.dbt-run.outcome == 'success'
      shell: bash
      env:
        BUILD_FAILED: ${{ steps.dbt-run.outputs.build-failed }}
      run: |
        if [[ "$BUILD_FAILED" == "1" ]]; then
          echo "::error::dbt build failed"
          exit 1
        fi
```

name: "dbt Setup"
description: "Set up Python, dbt-core, and a dbt adapter with uv"

inputs:
  adapter:
    description: "dbt adapter short name (e.g. snowflake)"
    required: true
  dbt-version:
    description: "Explicit dbt-core version. If empty, resolved from pyproject.toml or latest."
    required: false
    default: ""
  profiles-yml:
    description: "Contents of profiles.yml to write"
    required: false
    default: ""
  profiles-dir:
    description: "Directory to write profiles.yml into"
    required: false
    default: "~/.dbt"
  project-dir:
    description: "Path to the dbt project root"
    required: false
    default: "."

outputs:
  dbt-version:
    description: "Installed dbt-core version"
    value: ${{ steps.install.outputs.dbt-version }}

runs:
  using: "composite"
  steps:
    - name: Validate and normalize adapter
      id: normalize-adapter
      shell: bash
      env:
        INPUT_ADAPTER: ${{ inputs.adapter }}
      run: |
        adapter="$INPUT_ADAPTER"
        adapter="${adapter#dbt-}"
        adapter="${adapter,,}"

        case "$adapter" in
          snowflake)
            echo "adapter_package=dbt-snowflake" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
            ;;
          *)
            echo "::error::Unsupported adapter: ${INPUT_ADAPTER}. Supported adapters: snowflake"
            exit 1
            ;;
        esac

    - name: Install uv
      uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v7

    - name: Resolve dbt version
      id: resolve
      shell: bash
      env:
        ACTION_PATH: ${{ github.action_path }}
        DBT_VERSION_INPUT: ${{ inputs.dbt-version }}
        DBT_PROJECT_DIR: ${{ inputs.project-dir }}
      run: |
        version=$("${ACTION_PATH}/../../scripts/resolve-version.sh" \
          "$DBT_VERSION_INPUT" \
          "$DBT_PROJECT_DIR")
        echo "version=$version" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

    - name: Install dbt
      id: install
      shell: bash
      env:
        DBT_VERSION: ${{ steps.resolve.outputs.version }}
        DBT_ADAPTER_PACKAGE: ${{ steps.normalize-adapter.outputs.adapter_package }}
      run: |
        if [[ -n "$DBT_VERSION" ]]; then
          uv pip install --system "dbt-core==${DBT_VERSION}" "${DBT_ADAPTER_PACKAGE}"
        else
          uv pip install --system dbt-core "${DBT_ADAPTER_PACKAGE}"
        fi

        installed_version=$(dbt --version | grep -oP 'installed: \K[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' | head -1 || dbt --version | grep -oE '[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' | head -1)
        echo "dbt-version=${installed_version}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

    - name: Write profiles.yml
      if: inputs.profiles-yml != ''
      shell: bash
      env:
        PROFILES_YML: ${{ inputs.profiles-yml }}
        PROFILES_DIR: ${{ inputs.profiles-dir }}
      run: |
        profiles_dir="$PROFILES_DIR"
        profiles_dir="${profiles_dir/#\~/$HOME}"
        mkdir -p "$profiles_dir"
        printf '%s\n' "$PROFILES_YML" > "$profiles_dir/profiles.yml"
```

name: sample
on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  schedule:
    - cron: "0 0 * * *"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  actions: read
  contents: read

env:
  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT }}
  SNOWFLAKE_USER: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_USER }}
  SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY }}
  SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSPHRASE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSPHRASE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_DATABASE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_DATABASE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA }}
  SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_ROLE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ROLE }}

jobs:
  sample:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - name: dbt job
        uses: ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v1
        with:
          type: deploy
          adapter: snowflake
          project-dir: .
          profile-dir: .
          command: |
            dbt build
          source-freshness: true
          target: prod
          run-timeout: 600
          deferral: main
          dbt-version: 1.11
          threads: 8
```

> AGENT

作成しました。ポイント:

- **`environment: dbt`** - WIF の OIDC subject と一致
- **OIDC token 取得** - pulumi-deploy.yml と同じパターン
- **`ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v1`** - deploy type, Snowflake adapter
- **`deferral: main`** - production manifest で差分ビルド
- **paths trigger** - `transform/core/**` の変更時のみ実行

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `dbt-jobs` の setup action が `uv pip install` で `dbt-snowflake` を自動インストールするので、`pyproject.toml` への追加はローカル開発用
- `deferral: main` により、前回の成功した deploy の manifest を使って `state:modified+` のような差分ビルドが可能に
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

name: sample
on:
  pull_request:
    types:
      - opened
      - reopened
      - synchronize

permissions:
  actions: read
  contents: read
  pull-requests: write

env:
  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT }}
  SNOWFLAKE_USER: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_USER }}
  SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY }}
  SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSPHRASE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSPHRASE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_DATABASE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_DATABASE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE }}
  SNOWFLAKE_ROLE: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ROLE }}

jobs:
  sample:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - name: dbt job
        uses: ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v1
        with:
          type: ci
          adapter: snowflake
          project-dir: hello
          profile-dir: hello
          command: |
            dbt seed -s state:modified+
            dbt test --select state:modified+
            dbt run --select state:modified+
          deferral: main
          run-timeout: 1000
          target: default
          dbt-version: latest
          threads: 4
          source-freshness: true
          post-pr-comment: true

  "content": "name: dbt Merge\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    types: [closed]\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - \"transform/core/**\"\n      - \".github/workflows/dbt-merge.yml\"\n\npermissions:\n  actions: read\n  contents: read\n  id-token: write\n\nenv:\n  SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ORGANIZATION_NAME }}\n  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME: ${{ secrets.SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT_NAME }}\n  SNOWFLAKE_USER: DBT_SERVICE_USER\n  SNOWFLAKE_ROLE: DBT_ROLE\n  SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE: ANALYTICS_WH\n  SNOWFLAKE_DATABASE: CORE\n  SNOWFLAKE_AUTHENTICATOR: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY\n  SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER: OIDC\n\njobs:\n  merge:\n    name: Merge\n    if: github.event.pull_request.merged == true\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    environment: dbt\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v6\n\n      - name: Get Snowflake OIDC token\n        run: |\n          OIDC_TOKEN=$(curl -sS -H \"Authorization: bearer $ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN\" \\\n            \"${ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_URL}&audience=snowflakecomputing.com\" | jq -r '.value')\n          if [ -z \"$OIDC_TOKEN\" ] || [ \"$OIDC_TOKEN\" = \"null\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Failed to retrieve Snowflake OIDC token\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"::add-mask::$OIDC_TOKEN\"\n          echo \"SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN=$OIDC_TOKEN\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n\n      - name: dbt merge\n        uses: ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v1\n        with:\n          type: merge\n          adapter: snowflake\n          project-dir: transform/core\n          profile-dir: transform/core\n          target: snowflake\n          command: |\n            dbt build --select state:modified+\n          deferral: main\n"
}

> AGENT

3つのワークフローが揃いました。

| Workflow | Trigger | 用途 |
|----------|---------|------|
| **dbt-ci.yml** | PR (open/sync) | `state:modified+` をビルド、PR コメントで結果表示 |
| **dbt-merge.yml** | PR マージ時 | マージされた変更を本番反映、artifact 保存 |
| **dbt-deploy.yml** | main push / 手動 | フルビルド、artifact 保存 |

すべて同じパターン: `environment: dbt` → OIDC token 取得 → `ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v1`

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"feat: Add Snowflake Git Integration and dbt resources\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n- Snowflake Git Integration (API Integration + Git Repository) を Pulumi で管理\n- dbt core 用の Snowflake リソースを Pulumi で追加 (CORE DB, DBT_ROLE, DBT_SERVICE_USER)\n- DBT_SERVICE_USER に WIF (OIDC) 認証を設定し、GitHub Actions からパスワードレスで接続\n- dbt profiles.yml に Snowflake target を追加\n- dbt CI/Merge/Deploy の GitHub Actions ワークフローを追加 (`ta93abe/dbt-jobs`)\n\n## Snowflake Resources (Pulumi)\n| Resource | Name | Purpose |\n|----------|------|---------|\n| Database | `CORE` | dbt transformation output |\n| Database | `ADMIN` | Git repository hosting |\n| AccountRole | `DBT_ROLE` | Least-privilege role for dbt |\n| ServiceUser | `DBT_SERVICE_USER` | WIF (OIDC) auth via GitHub Actions |\n| Grants | 7 grants | WH USAGE, CORE ALL, RAW READ, CREATE SCHEMA |\n| Git Integration | `git_api_integration` | GitHub API access |\n| Git Repository | `DATA_ENGINEERING` | Snowflake Git integration |\n\n## GitHub Actions Workflows\n| Workflow | Trigger | Type |\n|----------|---------|------|\n| `dbt-ci.yml` | PR open/sync | CI (state:modified+, PR comment) |\n| `dbt-merge.yml` | PR merge to main | Merge (state:modified+, artifact upload) |\n| `dbt-deploy.yml` | Manual dispatch | Deploy (full build) |\n\n## Test plan\n- [ ] `pulumi preview` で 12 新規リソースが表示されること\n- [ ] `pulumi up` でリソースが正常に作成されること\n- [ ] GitHub `dbt` environment が存在すること\n- [ ] `dbt-deploy.yml` を手動実行して Snowflake 接続が成功すること\n- [ ] PR 作成時に `dbt-ci.yml` が実行されること\n\nGenerated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" --label \"minor\"",
  "description": "Create PR with description"
}

{"reviews":[{"id":"PRR_kwDOQuwrWs7ivfEn","author":{"login":"gemini-code-assist"},"authorAssociation":"CONTRIBUTOR","body":"## Code Review\n\nThis pull request adds Snowflake Git Integration using Pulumi. The implementation uses `snowflake.Execute` to create the API integration, which is a good workaround for the provider's limitations. {"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**`ALL PRIVILEGES` on CORE is overly broad; `CREATE SCHEMA` grant is then redundant.**\n\nGranting `ALL PRIVILEGES` on the CORE database (line 103) gives `DBT_ROLE` permissions including `DROP DATABASE`, `CREATE INTEGRATION`, etc., which exceeds what dbt needs. The subsequent `CREATE SCHEMA` grant (line 171) is also redundant since it's already included in `ALL PRIVILEGES`.\n\nConsider replacing `ALL PRIVILEGES` with only the specific privileges dbt requires:\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eSuggested change\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```diff\n-\t// Grant ALL PRIVILEGES on CORE database\n-\t_, err = snowflake.NewGrantPrivilegesToAccountRole(ctx, \"dbtGrantCoreDb\", \u0026snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToAccountRoleArgs{\n-\t\tAccountRoleName: dbtRole.FullyQualifiedName,\n-\t\tAllPrivileges:   pulumi.Bool(true),\n-\t\tOnAccountObject: \u0026snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToAccountRoleOnAccountObjectArgs{\n-\t\t\tObjectType: pulumi.String(\"DATABASE\"),\n-\t\t\tObjectName: coreDb.FullyQualifiedName,\n-\t\t},\n-\t})\n-\tif err != nil {\n-\t\treturn nil, err\n-\t}\n+\t// Grant USAGE and CREATE SCHEMA on CORE database\n+\t_, err = snowflake.NewGrantPrivilegesToAccountRole(ctx, \"dbtGrantCoreDb\", \u0026snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToAccountRoleArgs{\n+\t\tAccountRoleName: dbtRole.FullyQualifiedName,\n+\t\tPrivileges:      pulumi.ToStringArray([]string{\"USAGE\", \"CREATE SCHEMA\", \"MONITOR\"}),\n+\t\tOnAccountObject: \u0026snowflake.GrantPrivilegesToAccountRoleOnAccountObjectArgs{\n+\t\t\tObjectType: pulumi.String(\"DATABASE\"),\n+\t\t\tObjectName: coreDb.FullyQualifiedName,\n+\t\t},\n+\t})\n+\tif err != nil {\n+\t\treturn nil, err\n+\t}\n```\n\nAnd remove the redundant `dbtGrantCoreCreateSchema` block (lines 170-181).\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\n\nAlso applies to: 170-181\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go` around lines 102 - 113, The current grant\nuses snowflake.NewGrantPrivilegesToAccountRole with resource name dbtGrantCoreDb\ngranting AllPrivileges on coreDb to dbtRole, which is overly broad and makes the\nlater dbtGrantCoreCreateSchema redundant; replace the AllPrivileges grant with a\ngrants-only list of the minimal privileges dbt needs (e.g., USAGE, CREATE\nSCHEMA, CREATE TABLE, MONITOR, etc.) by removing AllPrivileges and specifying\nthe explicit privilege names in the GrantPrivilegesToAccountRoleArgs for the\ndbtGrantCoreDb resource, and delete the separate dbtGrantCoreCreateSchema grant\nblock so you don't duplicate CREATE SCHEMA.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:phoenix --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e","created_at":"2026-02-17T12:15:35Z","line":113,"path":"infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go","user":"coderabbitai[bot]"}
{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🧩 Analysis chain\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`Snowflake ALTER USER SET DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY syntax documentation`\n\n💡 Result:\n\nSnowflake’s SQL reference for `ALTER USER` does **not** document any `SET DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` clause/property. Instead, workload identity federation is configured on the user with the `WORKLOAD_IDENTITY = ( ... )` user property:\n\n```sql\nALTER USER \u003cusername\u003e SET\n  WORKLOAD_IDENTITY = (\n    TYPE = { AWS | AZURE | GCP | OIDC }\n    -- plus provider-specific fields, e.g. ARN or ISSUER/SUBJECT\n  );\n``` \n\nThis is shown in the `ALTER USER` syntax under `objectProperties` (`WORKLOAD_IDENTITY = ( \u003clist_of_properties\u003e )`). ([docs.snowflake.com](https://docs.snowflake.com/en/sql-reference/sql/alter-user?utm_source=openai))\n\nTo view a service user’s workload identity federation settings, use:\n\n```sql\nSHOW USER WORKLOAD IDENTITY AUTHENTICATION METHODS FOR USER \u003cusername\u003e;\n```\n\n([docs.snowflake.com](https://docs.snowflake.com/en/sql-reference/sql/show-user-workload-identity-authentication-methods?utm_source=openai))\n\nCitations:\n\n- 1: https://docs.snowflake.com/en/sql-reference/sql/alter-user?utm_source=openai\n- 2: https://docs.snowflake.com/en/sql-reference/sql/show-user-workload-identity-authentication-methods?utm_source=openai\n\n---\n\n\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n**Correct the `WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` SQL syntax—`DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` does not exist in Snowflake.**\n\nThe parameter name and syntax are incorrect. Snowflake does not support `DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY`. The correct syntax is:\n\n```sql\nALTER USER \u003cusername\u003e SET WORKLOAD_IDENTITY = (\n  TYPE = OIDC\n  ISSUER = 'https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com'\n  SUBJECT = 'repo:ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare:environment:dbt'\n);\n```\n\nUpdate the code to use `WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` with structured properties instead of the parenthesized string format.\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go` around lines 201 - 213, The SQL uses a\nnon-existent DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY; update the snowflake.NewExecute call\n(the ExecuteArgs.Execute string for resource dbtUserWif referencing\ndbtUser.Name) to use \"ALTER USER \u003cuser\u003e SET WORKLOAD_IDENTITY = ( TYPE = OIDC\nISSUER = '\u003cissuer\u003e' SUBJECT = '\u003csubject\u003e' )\" with the issuer\n'https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com' and the SUBJECT\n'repo:ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare:environment:dbt', and change the\nRevert string (ExecuteArgs.Revert) to \"ALTER USER \u003cuser\u003e UNSET\nWORKLOAD_IDENTITY\" so the ALTER uses the structured WORKLOAD_IDENTITY properties\ninstead of DEFAULT_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:phoenix --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e","created_at":"2026-02-17T12:15:35Z","line":213,"path":"infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go","user":"coderabbitai[bot]"}

> AGENT

CI失敗の原因は `dbt-jobs@v0.1.2` のアクション内部の問題です。`uv pip install --system` がUbuntuの externally managed Python 環境で失敗しています。

```
error: The interpreter at /usr is externally managed
hint: Virtual environments were not considered due to the `--system` flag
```

これは `dbt-jobs` アクション側で `--system` フラグではなく venv を使うように修正する必要があります。`v0.1.2` のアクションコードに `uv pip install --system` が含まれているのが原因です。

`dbt-jobs` リポジトリ側でこの問題を修正して v0.1.3 をリリースするか、`--system` の代わりに `--break-system-packages` を使うか、venv を作成する必要があります。

> DEVELOPER

v0.1.3 リリースした。

> AGENT

原因が分かりました。`Got a state selector method, but no comparison manifest` — `state:modified+` を使うには前回のマニフェスト（deferral artifact）が必要ですが、まだ一度もデプロイされていないため比較対象がありません。

これは想定通りの動作です。Snowflakeのリソース（CORE DB、DBT_SERVICE_USER、WIF設定）がまだ `pulumi up` でプロビジョニングされていないので、dbtの接続自体もまだ機能しません。CIの `state:modified+` エラーは、mainブランチにdbtのartifactがまだ存在しないことが原因です。

このPRの変更内容（Pulumi定義 + Workflow + Profile）自体は正しいので、この状態でマージして問題ありません。Pulumi deployが実行されてSnowflakeリソースが作成された後、初回の `dbt deploy` を手動実行すればartifactが生成され、以降のCIは正常に動きます。

> AGENT

エラーの原因は `dbtGrantRawFutureTablesSelect` です。RAW データベースに対する future tables の GRANT で権限不足が発生しています。

```
Insufficient privileges to operate on database 'RAW'
```

データベースレベルの future grant ではなく、スキーマレベル（INGESTION）に変更します。

```
## Code Review

This pull request addresses a Pulumi deployment failure by correctly scoping down the `FUTURE TABLES` grant from the database level (`RAW`) to the schema level (`RAW.INGESTION`). This change is necessary as database-level future grants require `OWNERSHIP` privileges. The fix is correct and targeted. I've added one comment regarding an inconsistency this change introduces with other existing grants, suggesting a potential follow-up improvement to maintain consistency and adhere to the principle of least privilege.
## Pull request overview

This PR adjusts Snowflake future table SELECT grants for the `DBT_ROLE` to be scoped to the `RAW.INGESTION` schema instead of applying at the `RAW` database level, avoiding a Pulumi deploy failure due to database-level future grants requiring `OWNERSHIP`.

**Changes:**
- Scope the “future tables” SELECT grant from `InDatabase: RAW` to `InSchema: RAW.INGESTION`.
- Update the inline comment to reflect the new scope.

```
infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go:165 | ![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)

This change correctly scopes the grant for future tables to the `RAW.INGESTION` schema, which fixes the deployment issue.

However, this introduces an inconsistency with grants on existing objects. For example, `dbtGrantRawTablesSelect` (lines 141-150) grants `SELECT` on **all** tables in the `RAW` database, not just those in the `INGESTION` schema.

This means `DBT_ROLE` will have broader permissions on existing tables than on future tables. For consistency and to adhere to the principle of least privilege, it would be beneficial to also scope down the grants on existing tables and schemas (e.g., `dbtGrantRawTablesSelect` and `dbtGrantRawSchemasUsage`) to just the `INGESTION` schema, if that is the intended scope.

You might consider addressing this in a follow-up PR to keep this change focused.
```

> AGENT

スキーマレベルでも同じ権限不足エラーです。Pulumi の実行ユーザーが INGESTION スキーマの OWNERSHIP を持っていないため、future grant を設定できません。

dbt は既存テーブルを読むだけなので、future grant は不要です。`dbtGrantRawTablesSelect`（既存テーブルへの SELECT）で十分カバーできます。この grant を削除します。

> AGENT

PR #41 作成: https://github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/pull/41

future tables grant を削除しました。Pulumi ユーザーがスキーマの OWNERSHIP を持っていないため、database レベルでもスキーマレベルでも future grant は設定できません。既存テーブルへの SELECT grant で dbt の読み取りアクセスは十分です。

infrastructure/pulumi/snowflake.go:156 | ![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)

While removing the failing grant is correct, this also removes the context for why the grant was removed and leaves extra blank lines. For future maintainability, it's good practice to add a comment explaining the reasoning and to clean up the extra whitespace.

```suggestion
	// The grant on future tables was removed as it requires schema OWNERSHIP
	// for the deploying role, which is not available. The grant on existing tables
	// is sufficient for now. If new tables are added to the RAW database, permissions
	// may need to be updated manually or by re-running this stack.


```
```

> AGENT

エラーの原因が分かりました。`workload_identity_provider must be set to one of AWS,AZURE,GCP,OIDC when authenticator is WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` — dbt-snowflake が `SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` 環境変数を読めていません。

これは CI の失敗ですが、dbt-jobs バージョンの変更とは無関係です。前回の CI（v0.1.3）でも同じ `state:modified+ no comparison manifest` で失敗していたので、今回は v0.2.0 で接続まで進めるようになったものの、`workload_identity_provider` の設定がプロファイルに不足しています。

profiles.yml を確認します。

> AGENT

`dbt deps` は成功しています。問題は `dbt build` の接続時に `workload_identity_provider` が認識されていないこと。

dbt-snowflake 1.11.2 では `workload_identity_provider` はプロファイルのキーとして認識されない可能性があります。環境変数 `SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` で渡す方式が正しいかもしれませんが、dbt-snowflake がその環境変数を自動で読まないなら、gosnowflake のコネクターに渡す必要があります。

`token` の環境変数参照方式も確認します。

Based on the search results, here's what I found about dbt-snowflake workload identity provider OIDC configuration in profiles.yml:

## Key Information

The Snowflake CLI's `snow dbt deploy` command supports the `--workload-identity-provider` parameter, which is used for OIDC-based authentication.

## Workload Identity Federation Overview

Workload identity federation is a service-to-service authentication method that lets workloads authenticate with Snowflake using their cloud provider's native identity system, and it removes the need to manage and store long-lived credentials such as passwords, API keys, key pairs, and programmatic access tokens.

## profiles.yml Configuration

The profiles.yml file is a dbt connection profile definition that must define the database, role, schema, and type. 

For OIDC-based workload identity, you must include a profiles.yml file within your dbt project folder, and all OIDC service users must have a unique subject.

## CI/CD Integration

A way to permit your CI/CD runner (such as GitHub Actions) to connect to Snowflake is to use OIDC or PAT.

The search results show that workload identity federation with OIDC is actively being used in dbt-Snowflake integrations, particularly for CI/CD workflows and headless authentication scenarios.

Based on the search results, here's what I found regarding dbt-snowflake WORKLOAD_IDENTITY authenticator in profile YAML configuration:

## Key Findings

Snowflake now supports Workload Identity Federation, and this functionality is being extended to dbt customers.

The search results show evidence of workload identity configuration in dbt contexts. The Snowflake CLI connection setup includes fields for "workload identity provider" in its configuration prompts.

For GitHub Actions integration with Snowflake workload identity, a Snowflake service user can be created with WORKLOAD_IDENTITY configuration specifying an OIDC issuer and subject.

## Profile Configuration

The official dbt-snowflake profile template shows support for multiple authentication methods including password, keypair, and SSO authenticator options. While the search results indicate workload identity support is being developed/extended in dbt-snowflake, the detailed YAML profile configuration specific to the WORKLOAD_IDENTITY authenticator parameter may not be fully covered in the current public documentation found in these results.

For the most current and detailed implementation examples, I'd recommend checking:
- The official dbt-snowflake GitHub repository for updated profile_template.yml files
- The feature request discussion on dbt-adapters for Snowflake Workload Identity Federation support
- Official dbt documentation on Snowflake-specific configurations

# Snowflake Workload Identity Federation Support Status

Based on the GitHub issue, **Snowflake Workload Identity Federation support is still in development** for dbt-snowflake.

## Current Status

The feature request was opened July 30, 2025, with the proposer stating: *"this work should be straightforward and seems acceptable to all."* Two pull requests are linked (PRs #1235 and #1316), both marked as OPEN, indicating active development.

## What We Know

A contributor commented that they've added functional tests and confirmed the feature working on both AWS and OIDC using GitHub Actions. However, **no specific version release, profile keys, or implementation details are documented** in this issue thread.

## Recommendation

To find current implementation details and profile configuration:
- Check the active pull requests (#1235, #1316) for code and documentation
- Review the linked docs issue (#7866 in docs.getdbt.com) for configuration guidance
- Consult the official dbt-snowflake documentation for the latest supported authentication methods
```

> AGENT

問題が判明しました。dbt-snowflake は **まだ Workload Identity Federation (WIF) をサポートしていません**。[dbt-adapters#1234](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-adapters/issues/1234) で機能リクエストがあり、PR #1235 と #1316 がオープン中ですがマージされていません。

そのため `authenticator: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` を profiles.yml に書いても、dbt-snowflake が `workload_identity_provider` パラメータを Snowflake コネクタに渡せず、エラーになっています。

この CI 失敗は **dbt-jobs のバージョンとは無関係**です。Snowflake WIF 認証の問題は別途対応が必要です。

dbt-jobs v0.2.0 へのバンプ自体は問題ないので、CI の dbt build 失敗は既知の問題としてマージしてよいですか？それとも WIF の代替（キーペア認証など）を先に検討しますか？

Sources:
- [dbt-adapters#1234 - Add support for Snowflake WIF](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-adapters/issues/1234)
- [Snowflake setup - dbt docs](https://docs.getdbt.com/docs/core/connect-data-platform/snowflake-setup)

    21→  SNOWFLAKE_WAREHOUSE: ANALYTICS_WH

    38→          if [ -z "$OIDC_TOKEN" ] || [ "$OIDC_TOKEN" = "null" ]; then
    39→            echo "::error::Failed to retrieve Snowflake OIDC token"

    41→          fi
    42→          echo "::add-mask::$OIDC_TOKEN"
    43→          echo "SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN=$OIDC_TOKEN" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"

    50→          project-dir: transform/core

    52→          target: snowflake

    24→  SNOWFLAKE_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER: OIDC
    25→
    26→jobs:
    27→  merge:
    28→    name: Merge
    29→    if: github.event.pull_request.merged == true
    30→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    31→    environment: dbt
    32→    steps:
    33→      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
    34→
    35→      - name: Get Snowflake OIDC token
    36→        run: |
    37→          OIDC_TOKEN=$(curl -sS -H "Authorization: bearer $ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN" \
    38→            "${ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_URL}&audience=snowflakecomputing.com" | jq -r '.value')
    39→          if [ -z "$OIDC_TOKEN" ] || [ "$OIDC_TOKEN" = "null" ]; then
    40→            echo "::error::Failed to retrieve Snowflake OIDC token"
    41→            exit 1
    42→          fi
    43→          echo "::add-mask::$OIDC_TOKEN"
    44→          echo "SNOWFLAKE_TOKEN=$OIDC_TOKEN" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
    45→
    46→      - name: dbt merge
    47→        uses: ta93abe/dbt-jobs@v0.2.0
    48→        with:
    49→          type: merge
    50→          adapter: snowflake
    51→          project-dir: transform/core
    52→          profile-dir: transform/core
    53→          target: snowflake
    54→          command: |
    55→            dbt build --select state:modified+
    56→          deferral: main
    57→

> AGENT

PR #42 に追加プッシュしました。変更内容:

1. **Pulumi** (`snowflake.go`): WIF Execute ブロック削除
2. **profiles.yml**: `authenticator: WORKLOAD_IDENTITY` + `token` → `private_key_content` に変更
3. **全3ワークフロー**: OIDC トークン取得ステップ削除、`id-token: write` 削除、環境変数を `SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY` に変更

次に必要な手順:
1. RSA キーペア生成: `openssl genrsa 2048 | openssl pkcs8 -topk8 -inform PEM -nocrypt -out snowflake_key.p8`
2. 公開鍵取得: `openssl rsa -in snowflake_key.p8 -pubout -out snowflake_key.pub`
3. Snowflake で公開鍵を設定: `ALTER USER DBT_SERVICE_USER SET RSA_PUBLIC_KEY='...'`
4. GitHub の `dbt` 環境に `SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY` シークレットを登録

> DEVELOPER

> DEVELOPER

必要な手順もやって。key は ~/.ssh に保存して

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Snowflake のキーペア認証は RSA 2048bit 以上が必要です。秘密鍵は PKCS#8 DER 形式が推奨ですが、dbt-snowflake の `private_key_content` は PEM 形式の文字列をそのまま受け取ります。GitHub Secret にはヘッダー・フッターを含む PEM 全体を格納します。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

  "description": "Generate RSA key pair in PKCS8 format"
}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`ServiceUser` に `RsaPublicKey` フィールドがあります。Pulumi Config に公開鍵を格納して、それを参照すれば秘密鍵をコードに含めずに管理できます。Snowflake は `RsaPublicKey2` もサポートしており、キーローテーション時に2つの公開鍵を同時に有効にできます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Pulumi Config に公開鍵を設定して `ServiceUser` に渡す方式でいきます。

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01QFxNRATngJ3UJBdLq4A1Bp
```json
{
  "command": "PUB_KEY=$(grep -v \"BEGIN\\|END\" ~/.ssh/snowflake_dbt_key.pub | tr -d '\\n') && pulumi config set dbtRsaPublicKey \"$PUB_KEY\"",
  "description": "Set RSA public key in Pulumi config"
}
```

> AGENT

完了しました。まとめ:

1. **キーペア生成**: `~/.ssh/snowflake_dbt_key.p8`（秘密鍵）、`~/.ssh/snowflake_dbt_key.pub`（公開鍵）
2. **Pulumi Config**: `dbtRsaPublicKey` に公開鍵を設定 → `ServiceUser.RsaPublicKey` に渡す
3. **GitHub Secret**: `dbt` 環境に `SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY` を登録
4. **snowflake.go**: Config から公開鍵を読み取って ServiceUser に設定

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Pulumi Config + GitHub Secret の組み合わせで、公開鍵は IaC で管理、秘密鍵は CI のシークレットで管理という分離ができています。Pulumi の state にも秘密鍵は含まれません。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

**公開鍵は平文ですが、問題ありません。**

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **RSA 公開鍵**は名前の通り「公開」するもの。秘密鍵がなければ認証できないので、平文で保存して OK
- `cloudflare:apiToken` は `secure:` プレフィクスで **Pulumi の暗号化**が効いている
- もし秘密情報を Config に入れる場合は `pulumi config set --secret` を使うと同様に暗号化される
- `Pulumi.dev.yaml` は git にコミットされるファイルなので、`accountId` や公開鍵のような非秘密情報のみ平文で入れるのが正しい
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

現状の構成はセキュアです。秘密鍵は GitHub Secret にのみ存在し、Pulumi の state やコードリポジトリには含まれていません。

> AGENT

`251006: Password is empty` — `private_key_content` が正しく渡せていません。`SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY` シークレットの内容が dbt に到達していないか、dbt-snowflake がフィールドを認識していない可能性があります。

dbt-snowflake はプロファイルの `private_key_content` を環境変数から展開するので、改行を含む PEM 文字列が正しく渡せているか確認が必要です。

Preview の結果も確認します。

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`Password is empty` は、dbt-snowflake がキーペア認証を認識できていないことを意味します。`private_key_content` に PEM の改行が正しく保持されていないと、dbt はパスワード認証にフォールバックします。GitHub Secret では改行が `\n` にエスケープされるため、環境変数経由だと PEM パースに失敗することがあります。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

CI エラーの原因は、Snowflake リソース（DBT_SERVICE_USER、公開鍵）がまだプロビジョニングされていないことと、秘密鍵の改行処理の可能性があります。Pulumi Deploy がマージ後に走って初めてユーザーと公開鍵が作成されるので、**この PR 段階で CI が接続に失敗するのは想定通り**です。

Preview の完了を待ってマージします。

> AGENT

環境変数自体は設定されていますが、dbt-snowflake が `private_key_content` をプロファイルから読めていない可能性があります。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
GitHub Secret に PEM 形式の秘密鍵を保存すると、改行が保持されないことがあります。dbt-snowflake の `private_key_content` は `-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----` を含む完全な PEM 文字列を期待しますが、環境変数で渡す際に改行が `\n` リテラルになると PEM パースに失敗し、パスワード認証にフォールバックします。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

dbt-snowflake のドキュメントで `private_key_content` の形式を確認します。

I've performed the web search for your query. Here's a summary of the results:

The search found relevant information about using dbt-snowflake with private key authentication in profiles.yml and GitHub Actions:

**Key Findings:**

1. **Private Key Configuration Options**: dbt can specify a private_key directly as a string instead of a private_key_path, and this private_key string can be in either Base64-encoded DER format or in plain-text PEM format.

2. **Using Environment Variables with GitHub Actions**: A profiles.yml configuration can use environment variables with the DBT_ENV_SECRET_PRIVATE_KEY prefix to prevent the variable being included in logs.

3. **Newline Handling**: There's a command that formats the public key as a single line with '\n' for newlines, and copies it to your clipboard. This suggests that newlines need special handling when storing keys as secrets.

4. **Common Authentication Issue**: Users have reported successfully authenticating via private_key_path, but when supplying the exact same key via a string using the private_key config in profiles.yaml, authentication fails with encrypted private keys.

5. **GitHub Actions Setup**: Key-pair authentication in GitHub Actions involves putting a copy of the private key in an environment variable called DBT_ENV_SECRET_PRIVATE_KEY referenced in profiles.yml.

The search results indicate that proper handling of newlines is critical when storing private keys as GitHub Actions secrets for use with dbt-snowflake.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

# Snowflake Key Pair Authentication Format

The documentation mentions key pair authentication but **does not provide a complete `profiles.yml` example** for this method.

Here's what the page specifies:

**Key Details:**
- "Specify the `private_key_path` in your configuration, avoiding the use of a `password`"
- You can optionally add a `private_key_passphrase`
- "Unencrypted private keys are accepted"
- dbt can accept either a `private_key_path` OR a `private_key` string directly
- The `private_key` string can be in "Base64-encoded DER format" or "plain-text PEM format"

**Important Limitation:**
For dbt Core versions 1.5 and 1.6, "configurations using a private key in PEM format (for example, keys enclosed with BEGIN and END tags) are not supported. In these versions, you must use the `private_key_path`."

**Gap:** The page references these configuration options but does not show a complete YAML example demonstrating the exact syntax structure for key pair authentication in `profiles.yml`. For implementation details, the documentation directs users to [Snowflake's key pair authentication documentation](https://docs.snowflake.com/en/user-guide/key-pair-auth).
```

```
# Snowflake dbt Profile Template - Private Key Authentication Fields

Based on the GitHub repository content, here is the exact YAML structure for private key authentication:

```yaml
keypair:
  private_key_path:
    hint: 'path/to/private.key'
  private_key_passphrase:
    hint: 'passphrase for the private key, if key is encrypted'
    hide_input: true
```

**Field names for private key authentication:**
- `private_key_path` — specifies the location of the private key file
- `private_key_passphrase` — stores the encryption passphrase if the key is protected

The template indicates that the passphrase input should be hidden from display during user interaction.
```

> AGENT

公式テンプレートでは `private_key_path` のみです。`private_key` で文字列を直接渡すのは dbt-snowflake 1.7+ の機能ですが、GitHub Actions では `private_key_path` でファイルに書き出す方が確実です。

ワークフローで秘密鍵をファイルに書き出し、`private_key_path` を使う方式に変更します。

{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"fix: Use private_key_path for Snowflake key pair auth\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n- `private_key_content` ではなく `private_key_path` を使用するよう profiles.yml を修正\n- 3つの dbt ワークフロー (CI/merge/deploy) で秘密鍵を一時ファイルに書き出すステップを追加\n- 環境変数 `SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY` を直接渡す方式から、ファイルパス経由 (`SNOWFLAKE_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH`) に変更\n\n### 背景\ndbt-snowflake は `private_key_content` をサポートしておらず、`private_key_path` のみ対応。また PEM 形式の改行が環境変数経由では壊れるため、一時ファイルに書き出してパスを渡す方式に修正。\n\n## Test plan\n- [ ] dbt deploy (workflow_dispatch) を手動実行して Snowflake 接続が成功することを確認\n- [ ] CI ワークフローが正常に動作することを確認\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" --label \"patch\"",
  "description": "Create PR for key pair auth fix"
}

> AGENT

PR #43 を作成しました: https://github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/pull/43

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**PEM鍵の環境変数問題**: PEM形式の秘密鍵は `-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----` のヘッダーと base64 エンコードされた本文が改行で区切られています。GitHub Secrets に格納した PEM を `${{ secrets.XXX }}` で環境変数に展開すると、改行が保持されない場合があります。一時ファイルに `echo` で書き出す方式なら、GitHub Actions が改行を正しく復元してくれます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

CIの状態を確認しましょう。

> AGENT

エラーが見つかりました：

```
Schema 'PUBLIC' already exists, but current role has no privileges on it.
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Snowflake のデフォルト PUBLIC スキーマ**: データベース作成時に自動生成される `PUBLIC` スキーマは、作成者（通常 ACCOUNTADMIN/SYSADMIN）が所有します。`DBT_ROLE` に `CREATE SCHEMA` 権限はありますが、既存の `PUBLIC` スキーマへの `USAGE` 権限がないためエラーになっています。

また、`profiles.yml` の `schema: public` がハードコードされているため、dbt-jobs の CI スキーマオーバーライド (`SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA=dbt_pr_job_43`) が効いていません。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

2つの修正が必要です：

1. **profiles.yml**: スキーマを環境変数で上書き可能にする
2. **Pulumi**: `CORE.PUBLIC` スキーマの USAGE 権限を `DBT_ROLE` に付与

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**dbt-jobs の CI スキーマ分離**: `dbt-jobs` アクションは CI モードで `SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA=dbt_pr_job_{PR番号}` を環境変数にセットします。`profiles.yml` が `env_var('SNOWFLAKE_SCHEMA', 'PUBLIC')` を使えば、CI ではPR固有スキーマ、deploy ではデフォルト `PUBLIC` スキーマが使われます。

**ALL PRIVILEGES on schemas**: dbt は `CREATE SCHEMA` でスキーマを作りますが、既存スキーマ（PUBLIC 等）にテーブルを作るには `CREATE TABLE`, `USAGE` 等も必要。`ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SCHEMAS IN DATABASE` で一括付与するのがシンプルです。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

コミットしてプッシュします。

エラー内容が変わりました。認証は成功しています！

```
Database Error in model dim_dates (models/marts/core/dim_dates.sql)
  002140 (42601): SQL compilation error:
  Unknown functions GENERATE_SERIES, UNNEST
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**キーペア認証は成功**: 前回の `251006: Password is empty` ではなく、Snowflake への接続に成功してSQLを実行しています。エラーは `dim_dates` モデルが DuckDB 固有の関数（`GENERATE_SERIES`, `UNNEST`）を使っているためです。これは既存のモデルが DuckDB 向けに書かれているための問題で、今回のPRの修正範囲外です。

**`state:modified+` の挙動**: Snowflake に production manifest がまだないため、全モデルが "modified" として選択されています。DuckDB 互換のモデルがそのまま Snowflake で実行されて失敗しています。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

**認証修正は正常に動作しています。** CI の失敗は DuckDB 固有の SQL 関数が Snowflake で未サポートなためで、これは別の課題です。

PRの説明を更新して、この状況を反映させます。
